## 2026-10-29 M7.0a: remove features — done

Phase 7 starts by slimming the app down to what the team will actually use. Fully removed (code, routers, REST routes, pages, nav entries, shared schemas, `can()` actions + tests, queues/workers, env vars, e2e specs, deps):

1. **SAML SSO + SCIM**: `auth/saml.ts`, `routes/saml.ts`, `routes/scim.ts`, `lib/scim*.ts`, `sso`/`scimToken` routers, Security page, `findOrCreateSsoUser`/`ensureMembership` (SSO-only helpers), `@node-saml/node-saml`, `@fastify/formbody` (only existed for SAML's POST binding), e2e's `xml-crypto`/`@xmldom/xmldom`. `API_URL` env var dropped too — SAML ACS/metadata URLs were its only consumer.
2. **Public REST API v1 + API keys + webhooks**: `routes/api-v1.ts`, `lib/api-key.ts`, `lib/webhook-*.ts`, webhook queue + worker, `apiKey`/`webhook` routers, Developer page. `lib/task-mutations.ts` existed only for REST v1, so it went too; `triggerWebhooksForEvent` calls removed from `task.ts`.
3. **Importers**: CSV import lib/runner/queue/worker, `routes/imports.ts`, `import` router, Import page.
4. **GitHub PR links**: `pr-link` router, `github-client.ts`, task-panel PR section, `GITHUB_API_BASE_URL` env var and the e2e GitHub mock server.
5. **Tauri desktop app**: `apps/desktop` deleted, `dev:desktop`/`build:desktop` scripts and `.prettierignore` entries removed (CI never built it).
6. **Goals/OKRs**: `goal` router, `goal-progress.ts`, Goals pages/components, goal invalidations in `use-realtime.ts`.
7. **Chat**: channels, private channels, threads, DMs, DM sound/toast, chat attachments, Brain-in-chat (channel/DM system prompts), `message` realtime entity, `message.created` notification label, `useRealtime`'s `onEvent` hook (DM-only consumer).

Kept, as specified: Slack notify, automations, Google Drive picker, docs + Brain in docs, Copywriter, Library, dashboards, all task features.

### Decisions

- **Data export moved to the Admin page.** It lived on the Developer page, which is gone; export is admin-tier (`export:run`) like Admin, so it's now an "Export data" section there (`data-testid="admin-export"`, same `export-tasks-csv`/`export-workspace-json` links). `data-export.spec.ts` now navigates via Admin.
- **Attachments are task-only again.** `attachments.message_id` dropped; `presignUpload`/`confirmUpload`/`list` schemas now require `taskId`; `requireAttachmentTarget` takes `{ taskId }`.
- **Migration `0054_polite_elektra.sql`** (new, hand-ordered — drizzle-kit's generated version dropped `messages` CASCADE _before_ dropping the `attachments` FK, which would have failed on the now-missing constraint):
  - deletes chat-attachment rows (`message_id IS NOT NULL`) before dropping the column — **their S3 objects are left orphaned** in the bucket (harmless, no row references them);
  - deletes notifications for `message.created` activity (they'd link to channels that no longer exist); the `activity` rows themselves stay as audit history;
  - soft-deletes (`deleted_at = now()`) Brain conversations with `context_type = 'channel'`;
  - strips removed actions (`channel:*`, `message:*`, `dm:*`, `goal:*`, `apiKey:*`, `webhook:*`, `import:*`, `prLink:*`, `sso:*`, `scimToken:*`) from `custom_roles.grants/revokes` and deletes matching `space_permission_overrides` rows — otherwise editing an old custom role would 400 on the now-closed `WORKSPACE_ACTIONS` enum;
  - drops the 12 tables and the `import_source`/`import_status`/`task_pr_link_state` enums.
- **Postgres enum value `brain_context_type 'channel'` left in place** (per the 7.0a brief — dropping an enum value means recreating the type). The tRPC schema (`getOrCreateBrainConversationSchema`) now only accepts `task`/`doc`/`global`, so nothing creates it; `BRAIN_CONTEXT_TYPES` and `execute-tool.ts`'s context type still mirror the DB enum.
- `task.pr_linked` stays in the activity-label map so existing task history still reads correctly.
- Removed the PR-link half of `integrations.spec.ts` (Drive half kept as its own test); `profile.spec.ts` now checks the avatar in the workspace shell badge instead of in a chat message; `admin.spec.ts`/`role-templates.spec.ts` assert Roles/Admin instead of Developer/Import.
- Spec docs (PRD/ARCHITECTURE/DATA_MODEL) got a one-line "Removed in M7.0a" note under the title; ROADMAP's M7.0a line marked done.

### Verified

- `pnpm check` green (0 errors; web lint warnings down to 54 — fewer route files).
- `pnpm test`: shared 60, web 15, API 278 — all passing (API down from 335: the removed features' own unit tests went with them).
- `pnpm db:migrate` applied `0054` cleanly to an existing local DB with data: 59 → 47 tables, `attachments.message_id` gone.
- Full local Playwright run at CI parity (`CI=1`): **67/67 passed**, no retries/flakes (down from 80 specs — the removed features' specs went with them). Phase-2 smoke test green.
- Not verified on GitHub Actions (push is blocked — this machine's GitHub login lacks write access to the repo).

## 2026-10-29 M7.0b: drop Docker; Supabase + R2 + PM2 — done

Developers no longer need Docker. Dev runs `pnpm dev` (api + web + worker via turbo) against a Supabase `canvas-dev` project, an R2 `canvas-dev` bucket and Redis in WSL. Prod is PM2 + Caddy on an Oracle Always Free VM against Supabase `canvas-prod` + R2 `canvas-prod`. Containers survive only in CI (`services:` Postgres/Redis, plus a RustFS fake S3 for e2e).

- **Deleted** `docker-compose.yml`, `Dockerfile`, `fly.toml`. Root `dev` no longer runs `docker compose`; `start:api` renamed to `start` (`start:worker` kept).
- **DB connection** (`packages/db/src/connection.ts`, pure + unit-tested): TLS for any non-local host; with `SUPABASE_CA_CERT` (PEM text or a file path) the chain is verified, otherwise `rejectUnauthorized: false`. Pool size from `DB_POOL_MAX` (default 5; `ecosystem.config.cjs` sets api 5 / worker 3). ssl-related params (`sslmode` etc.) are stripped from the URL, because pg lets them override the explicit `ssl` object, so `?sslmode=require` would otherwise force system-CA verification and fail on Supabase's pooler cert. `drizzle.config.ts` uses the same helper and now loads the repo-root `.env` (drizzle-kit never did).
- **pgvector on Supabase: no new migration needed.** `0034` already has `CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS vector` with unqualified `vector(768)` types. As Supabase's `postgres` role it installs into `public`; if someone enabled it from the dashboard first (into `extensions`), the `IF NOT EXISTS` is a no-op and the type still resolves via Supabase's default `search_path` (`"$user", public, extensions`). Not verified against a live Supabase project (no credentials here).
- **R2 storage** (`storage.ts`): S3 client sets `requestChecksumCalculation`/`responseChecksumValidation: "WHEN_REQUIRED"`. Newer AWS SDKs add CRC checksum params to presigned PUTs by default, which a browser upload can't satisfy; this is Cloudflare's documented fix. `ensureBucketExists()` now only creates the bucket and applies CORS for a **localhost** endpoint (CI's fake S3). For R2 (dev and prod) it only does a `HeadBucket` and logs a warning if unreachable: buckets and CORS are dashboard-managed (the app's token is object-scoped). CORS JSON documented in LOCAL_SETUP/DEPLOYMENT with explicit `AllowedHeaders: ["content-type"]`.
- **Redis**: default `redis://127.0.0.1:6379`; `REDIS_URL` removed from the production localhost guard (prod Redis is on the same VM). DB/S3/WEB_URL/Google redirect localhost + dev S3 creds are still rejected.
- **Fixed: `.env` loaded after the DB pool was created.** `index.ts`/`worker.ts` imported `@canvas/db` before `./env`, and `packages/db/src/client.ts` builds its pool from `process.env` at import time, so a `DATABASE_URL` that existed only in `.env` was silently ignored (localhost default used). Invisible while `.env` matched the Docker defaults; fatal with Supabase. New side-effect module `apps/api/src/load-env.ts` is now the first import of both entrypoints (confirmed in the built bundles: dotenv runs before `new Pool`). `playwright.config.ts` loads the same root `.env` for the two specs that query the DB directly.
- **Fixed: the built API never actually ran.** `node apps/api/dist/index.js` crashed with `Dynamic require of "events" is not supported`. tsup inlines `@canvas/db` (`noExternal`), and `pg` isn't an `apps/api` dependency, so CommonJS `pg` got bundled into the ESM output. Pre-existing (reproduced on the 7.0a commit); the Docker image would have hit it too. Fixed by adding `pg` to `apps/api` dependencies, so tsup keeps it external.
- **Vite `envDir: "../.."`**: `VITE_SENTRY_DSN` used to arrive as a Docker build arg; the server's `pnpm build` now reads it from the root `.env`. Only `VITE_`-prefixed vars reach the bundle.
- **PM2**: `ecosystem.config.cjs` with `canvas-api` (API + built SPA, single origin) and `canvas-worker`, both `NODE_ENV=production`.
- **Backups**: `scripts/backup-db.sh` → `apps/api/scripts/backup-db.mjs` (plain `.mjs` so it runs with no build step). It runs `pg_dump --format=custom --no-owner --no-privileges` of the `public` + `drizzle` schemas (Supabase's own auth/storage schemas aren't ours to dump), gzips it, uploads `backups/YYYY-MM-DD.dump.gz` (Europe/Belgrade date) and prunes to the newest 14. Key is `.dump.gz` rather than the brief's `.dump`, since the object is gzipped. `.env` isn't shell-sourced because values like `EMAIL_FROM=Canvas <…>` break `source`. `PG_DUMP`/`PGSSLMODE` overrides supported.
- Admin page backup guidance rewritten for PM2/Supabase/R2 (`hosting: "vm_pm2"`).
- Docs: `.env.example` rewritten with where each value lives in the Supabase/Cloudflare dashboards; new `docs/LOCAL_SETUP.md` (Windows, first-timer, WSL + Redis + Supabase + R2); `DEPLOYMENT.md` rewritten for Oracle + PM2 + Caddy + cron backup + restore; CLAUDE.md Commands and ARCHITECTURE.md DB/Jobs/Storage/Deploy rows updated. DEPLOYMENT's old "in-process pub/sub" scaling caveat was stale (board realtime already uses Redis pub/sub); the real one-API-process constraint is the in-memory Yjs doc rooms, documented instead.

### Verified

- `pnpm check` green; `pnpm test`: db 7 (new), shared 60, web 15, API 278.
- `pnpm build`, then the built `apps/api/dist/index.js` + `worker.js` run from the repo root (as PM2 runs them): `/health` → `{"ok":true,"checks":{"api":"ok","database":"ok","redis":"ok"}}`, worker listening on all 6 queues.
- `pnpm db:migrate` through the new drizzle config.
- Full Playwright run at CI parity (`CI=1`, local CI-style services): **67/67 passed**, no retries. Phase-2 smoke test green.
- Backup script end to end, in a Linux container (node 22 + postgresql-client-16) against the local stack: uploaded a 396 KB dump, pruned 16 → 14 objects; the dump restored into a scratch DB with identical table/user/migration counts (47/395/55). The only restore error is the expected `schema "public" already exists`.

### Not verified (needs real accounts)

- Against a live Supabase project (TLS handshake, Session pooler, `CREATE EXTENSION` there) and live R2 (presigned PUT/GET, CORS). Both are covered by config and docs only. First real run will be when `.env` is pointed at `canvas-dev`.
- PM2 itself and Caddy (not installed on this machine); Oracle VM steps are M7.10.
- CI changes on GitHub Actions (push still blocked).

## 2026-10-29 M7.1: People roles, reporting lines, staff profiles — done

### Built

- **Schema** (migrations `0055` + `0056`, split in two so drizzle-kit didn't need an interactive "rename or create?" prompt): `people_role` enum (`super_admin`/`hr`/`operation_manager`/`staff_manager`/`staff`) on `memberships`, default `staff`; `memberships.manager_user_id` ("reports to", FK users, `on delete set null`); new `employee_profiles` table (1:1 with membership: phone, address, date of birth, job title, department, employment start, emergency contact name/phone, `status` active/inactive, `deactivated_at`). `0055` copies every `is_operations_manager = true` (non-owner) membership into `people_role = 'operation_manager'`; `0056` drops the flag. Local DB: 10 flagged → 10 operation managers.
- **Owner ⇒ super_admin** always, via `effectivePeopleRole()` (shared), whatever is stored. It's computed rather than stored so ownership transfer can't leave a stale role behind.
- **Permissions**: `can()` takes a second resource type, `{ type: "people", peopleRole, target? }`, with its own closed action set `PEOPLE_ACTIONS` (people:viewDirectory/viewProfile/manageStaff/assignSuperAdmin, leave:approve/recordForOthers, payroll:view/manage, contract:manage, report:viewTeam, performance:view, holiday:manage, tasks:manageTeam). These are decided by people role, not membership rank, and are **not** grantable via custom roles. Team-scoped actions let a `staff_manager` act only when `target.isDirectReport` (`assertCanPeople` resolves this from `manager_user_id`); a list-level check passes them, and the caller scopes with `teamScopeFor()`. A staff manager can't use a team action on themselves; anyone can view their own profile. `people:assignSuperAdmin` (super_admin only) guards granting/removing/deactivating a super admin, so HR can't mint a peer above themselves. Full role × action matrix unit-tested (`can.test.ts`, 18 new tests) plus `wouldCreateReportingCycle`/`effectivePeopleRole` (shared).
- **Deactivation**: `lib/membership.ts`'s active-only helpers (`getMembershipRole`/`getMembershipContext`/`getPeopleContext`) treat an inactive profile as a non-member, so every `assertCan`/`can()` fails for that workspace. `workspace.listMine` hides the workspace, `workspace.members` (every picker) hides the member, assigning them is refused, notifications (`notifyWorkspace`, ops managers) skip them. Membership, tasks, comments and activity are untouched; reactivating restores access as before. Raw `findMembership()` is used only where an inactive member must still be found (HR actions, invite re-accept, owner/removal guards). The owner and yourself can't be deactivated.
- **`people` router**: `myAccess` (every workspace + people action the caller holds, which drives the UI), `directory`, `profile` (personal fields only for HR-side roles and the person themselves), `createStaff`, `updateProfile`, `setRole`, `setManager` (active member, no reporting loops), `setStatus`. Every mutation writes an `activity` row (`person.created/profile_updated/role_changed/manager_changed/deactivated/reactivated`, entity type `person`).
- **HR creates accounts directly** (name, email, initial password, role, reports-to, profile fields). An email that already has an account is refused with "invite them instead": attaching someone's existing account without their consent is the takeover shape follow-up #29 closed for SSO.
- **`is_operations_manager` → people role**: `notifyOperationsManagers` now reads `people_role = 'operation_manager'`. The Members panel's Ops Manager checkbox and `workspace.setOperationsManager` are gone; the panel shows a people-role badge instead. Roles are set on the People profile.
- **UI**: "People" nav group → Directory (virtualized, search, active/all filter, Add staff form for HR). Profile page shows details; for HR it also has the role + reports-to editor, profile edit form and deactivate/reactivate. Nav entries are now filtered by `people.myAccess` (each role only sees sections it can use: Home/Workload need `report:viewTeam`, Admin `workspace:manage`, Roles `customRole:view`, Directory `people:viewDirectory`, content sections their `*:view` action). The server still checks every call.
- **Landing**: Home is the team view. Managers (super_admin/hr/operation_manager/staff_manager) land there; `staff` are redirected to the new **My Tasks** page (`task.listMine`: every task/subtask assigned to me across the workspace, grouped by status kind, soonest due first, virtualized, live via realtime). My Tasks links to the user's own profile (staff have no Directory).

### Decisions

- Landing applies when a workspace is opened (`/w/:id`), not on the post-login workspace list. The list stays the multi-workspace hub; "Open" then lands each role correctly.
- Existing members all start as `staff` (the default the brief specifies), so existing non-owner admins/members now land on My Tasks until HR gives them a manager-side role.
- My Tasks groups by status **kind** (In progress / To do / Done / Closed): statuses are per list, so a cross-list page can't group by status name meaningfully.
- Directory is visible to every manager-side role (it's a phone book); personal fields (phone, address, DOB, emergency contact) are limited to HR/super admin and the person themselves.
- Existing specs updated for the new model: `ops-manager-finish-task` and `forms` set the operation-manager role via the People profile (new `setPeopleRole` e2e helper) instead of the removed checkbox. `admin.spec`'s deep-link bounce now ends on My Tasks, since the invited member is staff.

### Verified

- `pnpm check` green; `pnpm test`: API 296 (+18 People matrix), shared 65 (+5), db 7, web 15.
- New `people.spec.ts`: owner adds an HR account → HR signs in with the initial password (lands on the team view) → creates a staff manager and a staff member → sets the staff member's reports-to from their profile → staff signs in and lands on **My Tasks** with no Directory/Home in the nav → HR deactivates them → the workspace disappears from their list.
- Full Playwright run at CI parity (`CI=1`, retries off): **68/68 passed**.
- `0055`/`0056` applied to the local DB with data: 10 Ops Manager flags → 10 `operation_manager` roles.

### TODO / next

- `teamScopeFor()` is in place but no list-level team view consumes it yet. The leave/report/performance milestones (7.3, 7.5, 7.8) are its first users.

## 2026-10-30 M7.2: Task approval flow, proof photo, transfer, collaborators, due time — done

### Built

- **Schema** (migration `0057`): `status_kind` gets `'review'` (`ALTER TYPE … ADD VALUE 'review' BEFORE 'done'`); `tasks.due_time` (`time`, nullable) and `tasks.requires_proof` (bool, default false); `attachments.is_proof` (bool, default false); new `task_collaborators` (task_id, user_id; PK pair, cascade on both).
- **New lists** are seeded Pending (open) / In progress (active) / Awaiting approval (review) / Completed (done).
- **One rulebook for every status change**: `checkStatusTransition()` (`lib/task-transition-rules.ts`, pure, unit-tested) with a DB-backed wrapper `assertStatusTransition()` (`lib/task-transition.ts`). `task.update` (status picker, board drag), `task.bulkUpdate` (all-or-nothing: every row checked before any changes), `task.complete` and the new procedures all go through it. Rules: an assignee (or approver) moves to review, and when `requires_proof` is set they need at least one `is_proof` attachment first; only an approver moves to done/closed (approve, or close out directly); review → active/open goes only through Reject; only an approver reopens finished work; pending ↔ in progress is free.
- **Approver** = `tasks:manageTeam`, resolved per task (`isTaskApprover`): super_admin/hr/operation_manager pass; a staff_manager passes when an assignee reports to them; the task's **creator** passes via a new `target.isCreator` in `can()`, unless they're also an assignee, so nobody approves their own work. HR was added to `tasks:manageTeam` per the brief.
- **Procedures**: `task.submitForReview`, `task.approve`, `task.reject` (comment required, posted as a TipTap comment), `task.transfer` (replaces one assignee, note required and posted as a comment; the outgoing assignee may do it themselves, otherwise it takes an approver), `task.approvalState` (drives the panel), `task.collaborators.list/add/remove`. All write activity rows (`task.submitted_for_review/approved/rejected/transferred/collaborator_added/collaborator_removed`). Moving into done reuses the same side effects as `task.update`: `completedAt`, `task.completed` activity + ops-manager notification, status automations, realtime.
- **Notifications**: on submit, the creator and every assignee's direct manager (active, not the submitter, not an assignee); if that's nobody, the operation managers. Approve/reject notify assignees + collaborators; transfer notifies both people.
- **Proof photos**: `attachment.confirmUpload` takes `isProof` (images only); `attachment.setProof` marks or unmarks an existing photo (a "Mark proof" toggle on image attachments). The task panel's new **Approval** section shows proof photos at the top with an "Add proof photo" button (`capture="environment"` for phones), plus Submit / Approve / Reject (with a reason).
- **Create dialog** ("+ New task" on the list page): title, multi-assign, priority, due date + time, requires proof. The inline board "+ Add task" stays the quick path.
- **Due time** is shown wherever the due date is: task panel (editable, disabled without a date), table Due column, My Tasks, calendar chips and the public task form. `isOverdue()` (shared `due.ts`) compares against Europe/Belgrade wall-clock time via `Intl`, with no hard-coded offset; a date with no time is due by end of day. Overdue shows in red in the table and on My Tasks.
- **Collaborators** field in the task panel (add/remove chips) and a **Transfer to colleague** control under Assignees.
- My Tasks has an "Awaiting approval" group.

### Decisions

- **Only lists that have a review-kind status are gated.** Lists created before M7.2 keep their old free-form To Do/In Progress/Done behaviour, so nothing existing breaks; HR/admins can add an "Awaiting approval" status to an old list to opt it in.
- **Collaborators don't get extra access.** Seeing, commenting and attaching are already decided by workspace/space roles through `can()`. A collaborator is the explicit "keep me in the loop" list that approval/reject notifications reach. Granting access per task would need a per-task permission path, which `can()` doesn't have.
- Reject sends the task to the list's first **active** status (In progress), per the brief.
- Due time is stored as Kosovo wall-clock time (`time`, no zone) and shown as stored; only overdue checks convert "now" into Kosovo time.
- The public task form (`form.submitPublic`) still completes a task directly. It's a token link, outside the signed-in approval flow (unchanged behaviour).
- e2e specs that used the old default status names (To Do / In Progress / Done) were renamed to Pending / In progress / Completed. They run as the workspace owner, who is super admin and therefore an approver, so the gated moves still pass.

### Verified

- `pnpm check` green; `pnpm test`: API 307 (+10 transition rules, +creator case in can), shared 71 (+6 Kosovo-time due/overdue), db 7, web 15.
- `0057` applied to the local DB.
- New `task-approval.spec.ts`: manager creates a task in the dialog (assignee, priority, due 14:30, proof required) → staff signs in, sees it on My Tasks with its due time, can't submit without proof, uploads a proof photo and submits → manager sees it under Awaiting approval with the photo and approves → Completed on the board and Done on the staff member's My Tasks.
- Full Playwright run at CI parity (`CI=1`, retries off, local Postgres/Redis/RustFS containers): **69/69 passed**. Phase-2 smoke test green.

## 2026-10-30 M7.3: Daily reports + read receipts + 16:45 reminder — done

### Built

- **Schema** (migration `0058`): `daily_reports` (workspace, user, `report_date` date, TipTap `body_json`, `submitted_at`/`updated_at`, unique per workspace+user+date), `daily_report_reads` (report, user, `read_at`; PK pair) and `daily_report_reminders` (workspace, user, report_date, `sent_at`; unique triple), which is the reminder's idempotency ledger.
- **Pure helpers** (`packages/shared/src/daily-reports.ts`): `isReminderDue(nowUtc, tz, isHoliday?)` → `{ due, reportDate }`, `isWorkingDay(date, isHoliday?)` (Mon–Fri), `todayReportDate(now)`. All Kosovo time via `Intl` (`wallClock` from M7.2's `due.ts`), with no hard-coded offset. 9 tests, including both 2026 DST weekends (the Friday before and the Monday after, CET vs CEST), the Sunday change days themselves, the local-midnight rollover and the holiday hook.
- **`dailyReport` router**: `today`, `save` (always today's Kosovo date; the date is never client-supplied, so a report locks at midnight without any job closing it; empty reports refused), `mine` (history + read counts), `team` (date → who submitted / who didn't, with read counts), `get` (body + "Read by" list), `markRead`. Authors read their own; anyone else needs `report:viewTeam` over the author through `assertCanPeople`, so a staff manager is limited to their direct reports, and `team` is scoped with `teamScopeFor()` (its first consumer). Activity rows: `daily_report.submitted` / `daily_report.updated` / `daily_report.read` (first open only). New realtime entity `dailyReport` (`{entity, id, workspaceId, kind}`, no payload) refreshes the team view and receipts live.
- **Reminder** (`lib/daily-report-reminders.ts`, run on every existing scheduler tick): when due, finds active members with people role `staff` (never the owner) who have no report and no ledger row for today, claims the ledger row first (`ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`), then writes a `daily_report.reminder` activity row, sends the in-app notification and emails a link to the Daily report page. An email failure is logged and doesn't stop the rest. The notification shows as a standalone line and opens the Daily report page.
- **UI**: People → **Daily report** (everyone: today's TipTap form with Submit / Save changes until midnight, "Submitted at HH:MM · edited HH:MM", virtualized history with read counts); People → **Team reports** (`report:viewTeam`: date picker, "n of m submitted", virtualized Submitted/Not submitted rows); **report view** `/daily-reports/:id` (read-only body; opening someone else's report records the read; shows "Read by" with times). Times are shown in Kosovo time.
- Also fixed an M7.2 gap: the submitted/approved/rejected/transferred notifications weren't clickable in the bell. They now open the task.

### Decisions

- **Who is expected to report = people role `staff`.** The team view lists every in-scope staff member plus anyone else in scope who did submit; the reminder goes only to staff. Every role can still write a report from the Daily report page.
- **Reminder window = 16:45 until local midnight**, not exactly 16:45. A worker that was down at 16:45 still catches up that evening; the ledger keeps it to one reminder per person per day, however many ticks or workers there are.
- **The ledger row is claimed before sending.** If the email then fails, it isn't retried: at most once, not at least once. The in-app notification has already been written by then.
- **Holidays**: `isReminderDue`/`isWorkingDay` take an `isHoliday(date)` hook defaulting to "never". M7.4 passes the Kosovo calendar in at the one call site in `sendDailyReportReminders`.
- Read receipts are recorded by an explicit `markRead` mutation fired when the report view opens (a query never writes). Only non-authors count.
- `submitted_at` is the first submission; edits bump `updated_at` only.

### Verified

- `pnpm check` green; `pnpm test`: shared 80 (+9 daily-report/DST), API 307, db 7, web 15.
- `0058` applied to the local DB.
- Reminder job against the local DB with fixed clocks: nothing at 16:40; at Monday 16:50 Kosovo time 127 staff reminded (mock email); a second run reminded nobody (ledger and activity counts unchanged); Saturday reminded nobody. The test rows were deleted afterwards.
- New `daily-reports.spec.ts`: owner adds a staff member → Team reports shows him "Not submitted" → staff signs in (no Team reports in his nav), writes and submits today's report (stays editable) → manager's team view flips to "Submitted · Unread" live → manager opens it (read recorded) → team view shows "Read by 1" → staff opens his own report and sees the manager under "Read by".
- Full Playwright run at CI parity (`CI=1`, retries off, local Postgres/Redis/RustFS containers): **70/70 passed**. Phase-2 smoke test green.

## 2026-10-30 M7.4: Kosovo public holidays — done

### Built

- **Schema** (migration `0059`): `public_holidays` (workspace, `date`, `name`, `observed_date` nullable, `is_recurring_fixed`, `created_by`, soft delete via `deleted_at`), `public_holiday_notices` (the notice idempotency ledger: unique workspace + holiday + observed date), and on `workspaces`: `holiday_notice_days` (default 2, 0 = off) and `holidays_seeded_at`.
- **Pure calendar logic** (`packages/shared/src/holidays.ts`, 15 tests):
  - `KOSOVO_FIXED_HOLIDAYS`, the 8 dates from the brief.
  - `KOSOVO_MOVABLE_HOLIDAYS`: the Easter Mondays come with a suggested date from `catholicEaster()` / `orthodoxEaster()` (Gregorian and Julian computus; checked against 2024–2027); the Eids have none.
  - `expandHolidays(rows, year)` applies the **observed rule**: a Sat/Sun holiday moves to the next working day that isn't already a holiday or another holiday's substitute. Tested with New Year 2022 (Sat + Sun → Mon + Tue) and 1 May 2027 (Sat, and Mon 3 May is Orthodox Easter Monday → Tue 4 May).
  - Also `makeHolidayChecker` (expands years lazily and caches them), `isWorkingDay`, `countWorkingDays(from, to)` (inclusive; tested across a year boundary), `isHolidayNoticeDue`, `hasMovableHolidays`.
  - `isWorkingDay` moved here from `daily-reports.ts`; there is one copy.
- **`apps/api/src/lib/working-days.ts`** for 7.5: `loadHolidayRows`, `loadHolidayChecker`, `isWorkingDay(workspaceId, date)`, `countWorkingDays(workspaceId, from, to)`, plus the seeding helpers.
- **Seeding**: the 8 fixed holidays (recurring) are seeded when a workspace is created. The scheduler tick backfills any workspace with `holidays_seeded_at` null, which covers every workspace from before M7.4. The marker is claimed in the same transaction as the insert, so it seeds once and never re-seeds a calendar HR emptied on purpose.
- **`holiday` router**: `list` (any active member; the year's occurrences with observed dates, the "has movable" flag, notice days, `canManage`), `create` / `update` / `delete` (soft) and `setNoticeDays`, all `holiday:manage` (HR + super admin) with activity rows (`holiday.created/updated/deleted`, `workspace.holiday_notice_days_changed`). A recurring holiday can't take an observed-date override.
- **Holiday notices** (`lib/holiday-notices.ts`, on the scheduler tick): for this year and next, any holiday whose day off is between N days away and tomorrow → claim the ledger row → one `holiday.upcoming` activity row (attributed to the workspace owner, since activity needs an actor) → in-app notification + email to every active member. The bell shows "Public holiday: <name> — day off <date>" and opens the Holidays page.
- **7.3 reminder wired**: `sendDailyReportReminders` still uses `isReminderDue` for weekend and time of day, then skips any workspace whose calendar has that date off (one checker per workspace per tick).
- **UI**: People → **Holidays** (everyone): year switcher; a virtualized list with date, name, "Every year" / "<year> only" and "Day off …" when a holiday moved; an amber **"No movable holidays entered for <year>"** banner. HR also gets Add holiday, one-click "+ Catholic Easter Monday" / "+ Orthodox Easter Monday" (date prefilled) / "+ Eid al-Fitr" / "+ Eid al-Adha" buttons for whichever are missing that year, an optional "Day off (if different)" override for one-off holidays, edit/delete on hover, and the notice-days setting.

### Decisions

- **`observed_date` is an HR override, not a cache.** Null means "by the rule", computed per year at read time. A recurring row's observed day differs every year, and a movable holiday added later can bump another holiday's substitute day (the 2027 Labour Day case), so a stored value would go stale. Overrides are allowed on one-off holidays only.
- A recurring holiday's `date` only anchors month/day; the anchor year is the year it was seeded.
- **Notices go to every active member**, not only the `staff` role, because everyone gets the day off. A notice is timed by the **observed** day off; the email mentions the original date when they differ. It goes out from N days before until the day before (catch-up window), and the ledger keeps it to once per workspace and holiday. 0 days turns notices off.
- As with 7.3, the ledger row is claimed before sending, so a failed email isn't retried.

### Verified

- `pnpm check` green; `pnpm test`: shared 95 (+15 holidays), API 307, db 7, web 15.
- `0059` applied to the local DB; the worker's tick backfilled all 673 existing local workspaces.
- Scheduler jobs against the local DB with fixed clocks: Christmas 2026 notice not sent on 22 Dec (3 days before), sent on 23 Dec (one per workspace), and a rerun on 24 Dec sent nothing new (same ledger and email counts). The daily-report reminder sent 0 on Fri 25 Dec at 16:50 and 143 on Thu 24 Dec. Test rows deleted afterwards.
- New `holidays.spec.ts` (pinned to 2027): the seeded calendar shows Independence Day etc., Labour Day (Sat) has its day off on Mon 3 May; "No movable holidays entered for 2027" shows; HR clicks "+ Orthodox Easter Monday" (date prefilled 2027-05-03) and adds it; the banner clears and Labour Day's day off moves to Tue 4 May; a staff member sees the same calendar with no edit controls.
- Full Playwright run at CI parity (`CI=1`, retries off, local Postgres/Redis/RustFS containers): **71/71 passed**. Phase-2 smoke test green.

## 2026-10-30 M7.5: Leave management — done

### Built

- **Schema** (migration `0060`): `leave_type` / `leave_status` enums; `leave_requests` (type, dates, `working_days` numeric, reason, status, `decided_by/at`, `decision_note`, `recorded_by`, `document_attachment_id`); `leave_balances` (workspace, user, year, `entitlement_days`, `carried_over_days`, unique per user + workspace + year); `workspaces.default_vacation_days` (default 20).
- **Rules** (`apps/api/src/lib/leave-balance.ts`, pure, 19 tests):
  - Only vacation consumes balance.
  - `computeVacationBalance`: used = approved, pending counted separately; remaining = total − used; available = remaining − pending.
  - Overlap with any pending or approved request of any type, both ends inclusive.
  - No end-before-start, no zero-working-day ranges, no request crossing 31 Dec.
  - Vacation can't exceed available.
  - `approvalBlocker` re-checks the balance at approval time.
  - `carryOverFrom` = max(0, available).
  - Writing the tests caught a real bug: `findOverlap` compared `undefined !== undefined` and so ignored every overlap for a new request.
- **Working days** come from M7.4's `countWorkingDays(workspaceId, from, to)`: weekends and observed public holidays excluded. They're computed server-side at request time and stored on the row.
- **`leave` router**:
  - `mine` (balance for a year, my requests, `canApprove`/`canRecord`) and `preview` (live working days, available days and the rule problem while the form is filled in).
  - `request`: pending; notifies the requester's manager + HR, falling back to the owner.
  - `decide`: approve, or reject with a required note; `leave:approve` over the requester, never yourself; notifies the employee.
  - `cancel`: the employee withdraws a pending request; HR can cancel pending or approved leave and the employee is notified.
  - `record`: HR records leave for someone, approved immediately, `recorded_by` set, employee notified.
  - `team`: requests in the caller's scope, each vacation request with the person's remaining balance.
  - `calendar`: who's off; everyone sees names and dates, the leave type only for people in the viewer's scope.
  - `balances`, `setBalance`, `setDefaultVacationDays`: HR (`leave:recordForOthers`).
  - `presignDocument` / `confirmDocument` for a supporting document.
  - Every mutation writes an activity row. New realtime entity `leave`.
- **Leave documents are private.** A doctor's note is an attachments row with no task, stored under `leave-documents/<workspace>/<id>/`. The `/uploads/:id` route now also runs `canViewLeaveDocument`: only the employee and whoever may approve their leave can download it, and before it's linked to a request only the uploader. Other task-less attachments (AI references) are unaffected because they're identified by key prefix. The confirm step checks the key matches this workspace + attachment id, and a document can only be attached once, by its own uploader.
- **Carry-over** (`lib/leave-carry-over.ts`, scheduler tick): every active member gets a balance row for the current Kosovo year with `carried_over_days` = carry from last year's row, set only where it's still null (`ON CONFLICT … DO UPDATE … WHERE carried_over_days IS NULL`). Effectively one query a tick once done. A `leave.carried_over` activity row is written when the carry is > 0.
- **PDF**: `GET /leave-documents/:id/confirmation.pdf` (approved leave only; employee or approver). Built with **pdf-lib 1.17.1**, added to `apps/api`, since the repo had no PDF library. One A4 page: workspace, employee (+ job title), type, dates, working days, reason, approved by/on, "recorded by HR" when applicable, a signature line, and a reference + generated time in Kosovo time. Characters the standard font can't draw (Cyrillic, emoji) become "?" instead of failing; Albanian ë/ç render. 2 tests.
- **UI** (People group):
  - **Leave** (everyone): balance cards per year (entitlement, carried over, used, pending, remaining); the request form with live "N working days · X available" and the rule message (submit disabled while there's a problem); an optional supporting-document upload; my requests (virtualized) with status, decision note, Cancel for pending, Download PDF for approved.
  - **Leave approvals** (`leave:approve`): requests waiting or all, with Approve / Turn down (reason required) and the person's remaining days. HR also gets **Record leave** (person picker + the same form) and **Balances** (virtualized table: entitlement override, carried over, used, pending, remaining, plus the workspace default).
  - **Who's off** (everyone): month grid of approved leave on working days, plus a list for the month.
- Bell: leave notifications open Leave approvals (new request) or My leave.

### Decisions

- **The default 20 working days is a workspace setting HR can change** (Balances tab). A per-employee entitlement override is a `leave_balances.entitlement_days` value; blank means the default.
- **Carry-over only from a tracked year.** A year counts as tracked if the member has a balance row for it, which this job creates. So the first year Canvas runs, and a new hire's first year, carry 0 instead of a phantom full year from before anything was recorded. No cap and no expiry, since the brief didn't specify one; HR can edit "carried over" by hand.
- **Pending vacation counts as spent at rollover**, so approving an old-year request later can't push that year below zero. If HR edits last year's numbers after the rollover, this year's carry isn't recomputed; HR adjusts it in Balances.
- **Approvers** follow M7.1's `leave:approve` matrix: super admin, HR, operations manager (everyone), staff manager (direct reports only). Nobody decides on their own leave, including HR recording leave for themselves.
- Pending requests count against the balance, so two pending requests can't together overspend it.
- Whole days only for now; `working_days` is numeric so half days can be added later.
- Leave crossing the new year must be split, because each request belongs to one year's balance.
- `working_days` is stored at request time; a holiday HR adds later doesn't rewrite existing requests.
- Who's off shows the leave type only to people who could see that person's leave anyway (sick leave is personal).

### Verified

- `pnpm check` green; `pnpm test`: API 328 (+19 leave rules, +2 PDF), shared 95, db 7, web 15.
- `0060` applied to the local DB.
- Against the local DB:
  - Carry-over on 30 Oct 2026 created a 2026 row for all 941 active members, each carrying 0 (2025 untracked); a rerun created nothing.
  - Simulating 1 Jan 2027 with 3 approved days in 2026, Sam carried 17; a rerun created nothing.
  - Leave-document access: an unlinked upload is visible to the uploader only; once linked, to the employee and the approver; task attachments untouched (null → normal path).
  - Simulated rows deleted afterwards.
- New `leave.spec.ts`:
  - The owner adds a staff member, who signs in (no Leave approvals in his nav) and sees 20 days for 2027.
  - A weekend-only range shows "no working days".
  - He requests Mon 1 – Fri 5 Mar 2027 ("5 working days"): Pending 5, Remaining 15. An overlapping range is refused.
  - The manager sees "20 of 20 vacation days left in 2027" and approves.
  - Staff's view updates live: Approved, Used 5, Pending 0, Remaining 15. The confirmation PDF downloads (`application/pdf`, `%PDF-`). Who's off shows him on 3 Mar 2027.
- Full Playwright run at CI parity (`CI=1`, retries off, local Postgres/Redis/RustFS containers): **72/72 passed**. Phase-2 smoke test green.

## 2026-10-30 M7.6: Contracts & company documents — done

### Built

- **Schema** (migration `0061`): `contract_kind` enum; `employment_contracts` (kind, `parent_contract_id` self-FK for annexes/amendments, title, start/expiry dates, `attachment_id`, `uploaded_by`, soft delete); `contract_expiry_notices` (ledger, unique contract + threshold + expiry date, so extending a contract re-arms its notices); `company_document_categories` (soft delete); `company_documents` (category, title, current `attachment_id`, soft delete); `company_document_versions` (every version including the current one, unique per document + version).
- **One private-upload path** (`lib/private-upload.ts`): `presignPrivateUpload` / `confirmPrivateUpload` / `requirePrivateAttachment` / `signedDownloadUrl`, the same presign → direct PUT → confirm flow as task attachments. The key prefix marks the kind of file: `leave-documents/`, `contracts/`, `company-documents/`. Confirm checks the key is exactly the one handed out for this workspace + attachment id and that the object landed. M7.5's leave documents now use it too, replacing their own copy.
- **One download rule for private files** (`lib/private-attachments.ts`, used by `/uploads/:id`):
  - Leave documents: as in M7.5.
  - Contract files: the employee whose **current** contract file it is, or HR (`contract:manage`). A file replaced since is HR-only.
  - Company documents: any active member.
- **Signed download URLs**: `contract.downloadUrl` and `companyDocument.downloadUrl` check permission, then return a 2-minute presigned GET. `getPresignedUrl` gained an optional file name, which sets `Content-Disposition: attachment` with the original name (RFC 5987 encoded).
- **`contract` router**:
  - `mine` (own documents only), `list` and `forPerson` (HR), each with `daysLeft`.
  - `presignUpload` / `confirmUpload`, `create` (an annex/amendment must point at one of the same person's contracts; a contract has no parent; the expiry can't be before the start; a file can only be filed once).
  - `update` (title/dates, or replace the file; the old attachment row is kept and the activity row records `previousAttachmentId`), `delete` (soft), `downloadUrl`.
  - All of it needs `contract:manage` except `mine`/`downloadUrl` for the employee's own documents.
- **`companyDocument` router**:
  - Everyone: `list` (categories + documents with current file and version count; a document in a deleted category shows under "Other"), `versions`, `downloadUrl` (current or a given version).
  - HR (new people action **`companyDocument:manage`**, super admin + HR): category create/rename/delete, `presignUpload` / `confirmUpload`, `create` (writes version 1), `replace` (new version row + the current pointer moves, in a transaction), `update` (title/category), `delete` (soft).
- **Expiry notices** (`lib/contract-expiry.ts`, scheduler tick):
  - Pure `contractNoticesDue(today, expiry)` in shared (5 tests, with `daysUntil`) gives every threshold (30, 7) we're at or inside of, up to the expiry day.
  - Each due threshold is claimed in the ledger; if anything new was claimed, one in-app + email notice goes to HR and super admins (never the employee).
  - A notice missed while the worker was down still goes out. A contract filed 5 days before it ends gets one message, not two.
  - The bell shows "Contract expiring: <name> — in N days" and opens Contracts.
- **UI** (People group):
  - **My contract** (everyone): own contract with its annexes indented, dates, expiry badge, Download.
  - **Contracts** (`contract:manage`): virtualized list of all contracts (search, "Expiring within 30 days" filter), "File a document" (employee, kind, parent for annexes, title, dates, file), and per row: inline expiry date, Download, Replace file, Delete.
  - **Company documents** (everyone): grouped by category (virtualized), Download, version history with downloads of older versions. HR also gets add document / add category, move to category, upload new version, delete.

### Decisions

- **Company documents get their own permission** (`companyDocument:manage`) instead of reusing `contract:manage`. Same roles today (HR + super admin), but the library isn't about contracts. It's added to `PEOPLE_ACTIONS` and the `can()` matrix test.
- **Contracts keep the old file on replace** (the brief asked for version history only on company documents). The old row stays for audit, reachable only by HR; its id is in the `contract.replaced` activity payload. There's no contract version list in the UI.
- **Expiry notices go to HR and super admins only**, not the employee (the brief: "notifies HR").
- **Downloads use signed URLs** from these pages, as the brief asks. The `/uploads/:id` stream route still exists for these attachments and applies the same rules, so nothing is reachable there that the signed-URL path wouldn't hand out.
- Size cap 50 MB for contracts and company documents.

### Verified

- `pnpm check` green; `pnpm test`: shared 100 (+5), API 329 (+1 matrix row for the new action), db 7, web 15.
- `0061` applied to the local DB.
- Against the local DB with fixed clocks:
  - **Expiry notices:** none at 31 days; one at 30 days; a rerun the next day added nothing. On 28 Nov the main contract got its 7-day notice and a late-filed annex got both thresholds claimed with **one** message. Only HR was notified.
  - **Contract file access:** Sam can open his own contract file, Tina (another staff member) can't, HR can; a replaced annex's old file is HR-only.
  - Test rows deleted afterwards.
- New `documents.spec.ts`:
  - HR files a contract (expiring 2030) and a salary annex for Sam, and a contract for Tina, then replaces the annex file.
  - HR creates a "Policies" category, adds "Leave policy", uploads version 2; the history shows v1 + current.
  - Sam (no Contracts link in his nav) sees only his contract and annex, never Tina's. Both download via signed URLs with the right file names (the annex as its replacement).
  - Sam sees the company document without management controls and downloads the current version.
- Full Playwright run at CI parity (`CI=1`, retries off, local Postgres/Redis/RustFS containers): **73/73 passed**. Phase-2 smoke test green.

## 2026-10-30 M7.7: Payroll — done

### Built

- **Schema** (migration `0062`): `payroll_records` (workspace, user, unique per workspace + user, `net_salary_eur` numeric(12,2), `id_card_number_enc`, `bank_name`, `iban_enc`, `account_holder`, `updated_by`, `updated_at`).
- **`lib/field-crypto.ts`** (7 tests):
  - AES-256-GCM with a fresh 96-bit IV per value, stored as `v1.<base64 iv|ciphertext|tag>`; the version prefix leaves room for key rotation.
  - Each value is bound to its row and field through authenticated additional data (`payroll:<workspace>:<user>:<field>`), so a ciphertext copied into another person's row, or into the other field, fails to decrypt.
  - Tests cover: round trip (incl. ë/Ç), fresh IV every time and no plaintext in the stored value, wrong key, moved row/field, tampered byte, truncated value, unknown version, wrong key size.
  - `maskLast4` gives "•••• 8906".
- **`PAYROLL_ENCRYPTION_KEY`** (env): 32 bytes, base64, validated at boot. Locally a public dev key is used when it's unset or empty (an empty line copied from `.env.example` counts as unset). In production the API refuses to boot when it's missing, the wrong length or the dev key. Added to `.env.example` and to `DEPLOYMENT.md`, with a warning to keep it apart from the server: the nightly backup is useless for payroll without it.
- **IBAN** (shared `payroll.ts`, 3 tests): `normalizeIban`, `isValidIban` (ISO 13616 mod-97; Kosovo `XK05 1212 0123 4567 8906` etc.), `formatIban`, `formatEur` (de-DE → "650,50 €"; `sq-XK` isn't in every ICU build and Chrome fell back to "€650.50"). The upsert schema normalizes and validates the IBAN server-side; the form checks it live.
- **`lib/payroll.ts`**: the only place that decrypts. `loadPayrollRows(workspace, { reveal })` returns every active member (with or without a record), masked unless `reveal`. A value that can't be decrypted shows as "[unreadable]" and logs only which user/field, never the stored value.
- **`payroll` router**:
  - `list` (`payroll:view`): the masked table.
  - `get` (`payroll:view`): one person in full, for the drawer.
  - `upsert` (`payroll:manage`): never on your own record; null clears a field.
  - `mine` (any active member): own record, masked to the last 4.
  - `audit` (`payroll:view`): the trail.
- **The audit trail:** every read (table, a person's record, your own pay details), every edit and every export writes an activity row: `payroll.list_viewed` / `viewed` / `self_viewed` / `updated` / `exported`. Payloads carry who and which fields (`fields: ["iban", …]`), the format and the row count, never a value. The `audit` procedure lists them with actor and subject names.
- **Exports** (`GET /payroll-export/:workspaceId/payroll.xlsx|pdf`, `payroll:view`, audited):
  - **exceljs 4.4.0** (new dependency): a frozen header, a € number format, the ID column forced to text so long numbers don't turn into exponents, and a total row.
  - **pdf-lib**: landscape A4 with the header repeated on every page, cells truncated to fit, "Confidential · generated … · page N", and a total row.
  - Both contain the full decrypted values. 3 tests (the xlsx parsed back; the PDF paginates 120 rows; unsupported characters survive).
- **Never over WS**: new realtime entity `payroll` is `{entity, id, workspaceId, kind}` only. The client invalidates only `payroll.list`, which only HR has cached (an uncached query isn't fetched), so nothing reaches managers or staff.
- **UI**:
  - **Payroll** (People nav, `payroll:view`): a virtualized table (employee + position, net salary, bank, masked IBAN, masked ID card, last updated) with search and a running total. Clicking a row opens the **edit drawer**, which reveals that record (audited) and is editable with `payroll:manage`. Also an **Audit trail** tab and Export Excel / Export PDF.
  - **My pay details** (everyone): own record, masked to the last 4.
  - Neither payroll query refetches on window focus, since each fetch is an audited view.

### Decisions

- **The table is masked; the drawer and the exports reveal.** Every person-level view of the full values is then one audit row naming that person, instead of every table load silently exposing everyone's IBAN.
- **Nobody edits their own payroll record**, HR included: a second person has to.
- **Reads done by queries write activity rows**, deliberately. It's the one place a query has a side effect: who looked at payroll is exactly what the trail is for. The payroll queries don't refetch on focus, to keep that trail meaningful.
- The audit trail is visible to `payroll:view` (HR + super admin). Payroll activity rows also appear in Admin's activity log, but they hold no values.
- Members with no record still appear in the table (blank), so HR can see who's missing.

### Verified

- `pnpm check` green; `pnpm test`: API 339 (+7 crypto, +3 export), shared 103 (+3 IBAN), db 7, web 15.
- `0062` applied to the local DB.
- Production boot guard with `NODE_ENV=production`: a missing, 16-byte or dev key is refused; a real 32-byte key boots. The built-bundle style ESM default import of exceljs works under Node 22.
- New `payroll.spec.ts`:
  - HR fills Sam's record in the drawer. A bad IBAN check digit is flagged and blocks Save; the valid one (typed lowercase with spaces) saves.
  - The table shows "•••• 8906" / "•••• 7890" and never the full IBAN; the total reads 650,50; reopening the drawer shows the full IBAN.
  - **The DB row holds only `v1.` ciphertext**: neither the IBAN's digits nor the ID number appears anywhere in it.
  - The xlsx (`PK…`) and PDF (`%PDF-`) exports download.
  - The audit trail shows the edit, the view and both exports, with no values in it.
  - Sam has no Payroll link, sees his own details masked, and gets 403 on the export URL. A staff manager (has Directory) also has no Payroll link and gets 403.
- Full Playwright run at CI parity (`CI=1`, retries off, local Postgres/Redis/RustFS containers): **74/74 passed**. Phase-2 smoke test green.

## 2026-10-30 M7.8: Performance report, Employee of the Month, Engagements board — done

### Built

- **Pure metrics** (`apps/api/src/lib/performance.ts`, 15 tests). Every comparison uses Kosovo wall-clock keys, the same rule as M7.2's overdue: a due date with no time is due by 23:59:59 that day.
  - `classifyTasks(tasks, { from, to, now })` evaluates as of the end of `to`, or now if the range hasn't ended:
    - **completed on time / late**: finished inside the range, judged against the deadline (an undated task is never late);
    - **overdue**: open at that moment and past its deadline;
    - **in progress**: open, not overdue, in an active/review status;
    - **not started**: the rest of the open tasks.
  - "Open at that moment" uses `completedAt`, so a task finished after the range still counts as open (and possibly overdue) at its end.
  - Also: `employeeOfMonthScore`, `rankEmployeeOfMonth`, `previousMonth` / `monthRange` (year ends, leap February), `engagementStatus`.
- **Schema** (migration `0063`): `employee_of_month` (workspace, `month` "YYYY-MM", `user_id` nullable, `score`, `breakdown_json` = `{ winner, ranking }`, unique per workspace + month).
- **`performance` router**:
  - `team` (`performance:view`; a staff manager is scoped to their direct reports by `teamScopeFor`): per-employee totals for a range.
  - `employee` (same, per target): totals, on-time/late completions per Monday-start week, the overdue task list.
  - `engagements` (`report:viewTeam`, same scoping): one card per active employee with open count, in-progress/awaiting-approval tasks, next deadline and status.
  - `employeeOfMonth` (any active member): last month's result.
  - Data comes from `lib/performance-data.ts`: assigned tasks and subtasks, excluding deleted tasks, lists and spaces.
- **Employee of the Month job** (scheduler tick): from the 1st (Kosovo date), every workspace without a row for last month gets one.
  - All active members are ranked. Only people with any tasks are kept in the ranking; the winner is the top qualifier.
  - A row is written even when nobody qualifies (`user_id` null), which marks the month as done. The unique key keeps it to once.
  - A win writes an `employee_of_month.awarded` activity row.
  - Locally it computed 967 workspaces in ~4.4 s; a rerun created nothing.
- **E2E test hook**: `POST /test-hooks/employee-of-month { workspaceId, month }` recomputes (replaces) one workspace's month. It's registered only when `E2E_TEST_HOOKS` is set (the Playwright webServer env sets it), and `env.ts` refuses to start production with it on, like `SAFE_OUTBOUND_ALLOW_PRIVATE`. The caller still needs `performance:view` in that workspace.
- **UI**:
  - **Employee of the Month banner** on Home and on My Tasks, because staff land on My Tasks, not Home. Visible to everyone all month: winner, month, on-time/late counts.
  - **Result panel**: the top-10 ranking with score, breakdown and "not qualified" flags. Only for whole-workspace performance viewers (super admin, HR, operations manager), and only on days 1–5 of the following month.
  - **Performance** (Work nav, `performance:view`): This month / Last month / custom range; a virtualized team table (total, on time, late, overdue, in progress, not started); a person's detail panel with a donut of the breakdown, a weekly on-time bar chart (reusing `components/charts.tsx`) and their overdue tasks linking to each task.
  - **Engagements** (Work nav, `report:viewTeam`): a live board, one card per employee with status (On track / Due today / Overdue), open count, next deadline and in-progress tasks (⏳ marks awaiting approval), plus search and a status filter with counts. Virtualized by rows of cards.
  - Live updates: `use-realtime.ts` now invalidates `performance.engagements` / `team` / `employee` on every task event. No new WS messages.

### Decisions

- **Employee of the Month formula** (from the brief, recorded here as asked): **score = 2 × (completed on time) + 1 × (completed late) − 2 × (overdue at month end)**.
  - To qualify: **at least 3 completed tasks** (on time + late) in the month.
  - Ties: **more on-time** first, then **fewer late**, then **whoever finished their last task earliest**.
  - "On time" = `completedAt` ≤ due date + due time in Kosovo time; no due date counts as on time.
  - "Overdue at month end" = still open at 23:59:59 on the month's last day and past its deadline (from `completedAt`, so finishing it later doesn't erase that).
  - Every active member is eligible, whatever their people role. Subtasks count like tasks; tasks in deleted lists/spaces don't.
- **Who sees the ranking:** people who see the whole workspace's performance (super admin, HR, operations manager). A staff manager's performance view is limited to their reports, so they don't get the full ranking. The server sends the ranking only to those roles; the days 1–5 window is applied in the browser (it's a display rule, not a permission). That's also what lets the e2e test pin the browser clock.
- "In progress" includes "Awaiting approval" (review), since the work isn't finished.
- **Engagements is a new route** rather than part of Workload: Workload is about counts per person; this is per-person live status.
- Performance views write no activity rows (they're reads, unlike payroll).

### Verified

- `pnpm check` green; `pnpm test`: API 354 (+15 performance), shared 103, db 7, web 15.
- `0063` applied to the local DB; the Employee of the Month job checked against it (see above), simulated rows deleted.
- New `performance.spec.ts`:
  - Seeds last month's history in the DB: Sam 3 tasks on time, Tina 4 late.
  - Calls the test hook (`{ userId: Sam, score: 6 }`). With the browser clock on the 3rd, Home shows the banner and the ranking (Sam, then Tina "4 late").
  - Performance → Last month shows Sam 3/3/0/0 and Tina 4/0/4.
  - Sam (staff) sees the banner on My Tasks without the ranking.
  - Engagements shows Tina Overdue (an in-progress task due 2 days ago) and Sam On track. Moving Tina's due date to next week in another tab flips her card to On track live over the existing WS invalidation.
- `apps/web/vite.config.ts` proxies `/test-hooks` to the API in dev (the route only exists when `E2E_TEST_HOOKS` is set).
- Full Playwright run at CI parity (`CI=1`, retries off, local Postgres/Redis/RustFS containers): **75/75 passed**. Phase-2 smoke test green.
